Rank p-1 owns high rows, gets the most work across all steps. So contiguous distribution imbalanced significantly. Cyclic distribution balances: rank r owns rows r, r+p, r+2p,... Good.\n\nDecomposition:\n\n```cpp\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, const int rank, const int size, const size_t localRows) {\n    const size_t p = (size_t)size;\n    std::vector<double> rowbuf(n);\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n        const int owner = (int)(j % p);\n        const size_t ownerRow = j / p;\n        double* bcastPtr;\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            double* row = &A[ownerRow * n];\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                sum += row[k] * row[k];\n            }\n            const double val = row[j] - sum;\n            if (val <= 0.0) {\n                row[j] = val;\n            } else {\n                row[j] = sqrt(val);\n            }\n            bcastPtr = row;\n        } else {\n            bcastPtr = rowbuf.data();\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(bcastPtr, (int)(j + 1), MPI_DOUBLE, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        const double diag = bcastPtr[j];\n        if (diag <= 0.0) {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", j);\n            }\n            return false;\n        }\n        size_t k0;\n        if ((size_t)rank > j) {\n            k0 = 0;\n        } else {\n            k0 = (j - (size_t)rank) / p + 1;\n        }\n        for (size_t k = k0; k < localRows; ++k) {\n            double* row = &A[k * n];\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < j; ++t) {\n                sum += row[t] * bcastPtr[t];\n            }\n            row[j] = (row[j] - sum) / diag;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n```\n\nPotential concern: When rank==owner, after Bcast root's buffer might be modified? Each column is broadcast so every rank can update its\n    // own rows; per-element summation order matches the sequential algorithm.\n    const size_t p = (size_t)size;\n    std::vector<double> rowbuf(n);\n\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n        const int owner = (int)(j % p);\n        const size_t ownerRow = j / p;\n\n        double* bcastPtr;\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            double* row = &A[ownerRow * n];\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                sum += row[k] * row[k];\n            }\n            const double val = choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, const int rank, const int size, const size_t localRows) {\n    // A holds this rank's rows: local row k is global row k * size + rank.\n    // Rows are owned cyclically, which gives each rank an equal share of the\n    // triangular work. Each column is broadcast so every rank can update its\n    // own rows; per-element summation order matches the sequential algorithm.\n    const size_t p = (size_t)size;\n    std::vector<double> rowbuf(n);\n\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n        const int owner = (int)(j % p);\n        const size_t ownerRow = j / p;\n\n        double* bcastPtr;\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            double* row = &A[ownerRow * n];\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                sum += row[k] * row[k];\n            }\n            const double val = row[j] - sum;\n            if (val <= 0.0) {\n                row[j] = val;\n            } else {\n                row[j] = sqrt(val);\n            }\n            bcastPtr = row;\n        } else {\n            bcastPtr = rowbuf.data();\n        }\n\n        MPI_Bcast(bcastPtr, (int)(j + 1), MPI_DOUBLE, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        const double diag = bcastPtr[j];\n        if (diag <= 0.0) {\n k0;\n        if ((size_t)rank > j) {\n            k0 = 0;\n        } else {\n            k0 = (j - (size_t)rank) / p + 1;\n        }\n\n        for (size_t k = k0; k < localRows; ++k) {\n int size, const size_t localRows) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n\n    A.assign(localRows * n, 0.0);\n    if (localRows == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t p = (size_t)size;\n    std::vector<double> Blocal(localRows * n);\n bool keep = ((j % p) == (size_t)rank);\n        double* dst = keep ? &Blocal[(j / p) * n] : nullptr;\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < n; ++t) {\n            const double value = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n            if (keep) {\n                dst[t] = value;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Compute this rank's owned rows of A = B * B^T\n    seed = 42;\n    for        const double* Bi = &Blocal[k * n];\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < n; ++t) {\n                sum += Bi[t] * Bj[t];\n            }\n            A[k * n + j] =
